     1.    鋰資源市場分析                                          1.   Analysis of the lithium resource market

           全球鋰資源供給大部分來自鹽湖和硬岩                                     The majority of global lithium resources are sourced from salars and
           鋰礦。成熟的鹽湖主要分佈在南美鋰三                                     hard rock lithium mines. Well-developed salars are mainly found in the
           角地區（智利、阿根廷及玻利維亞）和中                                    South America Lithium Triangle region (Chile, Argentina and Bolivia)
           國，大部分鋰礦山依然集中在澳大利                                      and in China, while the majority of lithium mines are concentrated
           亞。近年來，在終端市場的需求刺激                                      in Australia. In recent years, stimulated by demand in the end-user
           下，鋰資源的投資開發力度加大，供                                      market, there has been an increased investment and development
           應逐漸多元化。根據長江證券研究所                                      in lithium resources, leading to a diversification of supply. According
           的數據，2026年預計全球供給鋰資源                                    to data from Yangtze Securities Research Institute, global lithium
           197.5萬噸LCE（碳酸鋰當量），同比增長                                resource supply is expected to reach 1,975,000 tons of LCE (lithium
           27%，其中鋰輝石、鹽湖及鋰雲母來源                                    carbonate equivalent) in 2026, representing a year-on-year increase
           分別是106.5萬噸LCE、74.7萬噸LCE及                              of 27%, among which 1,065,000 tons of LCE, 747,000 tons of LCE
           16.3萬噸LCE，分別佔比54%、38%及                                and 163,000 tons of LCE are sourced from spodumene, salars and
           8%。                                                   lepidolite, accounting for 54%, 38% and 8%, respectively.

行業回顧（續）                                   INDUSTRY REVIEW (Continued)
1.   鋰資源市場分析（續）                           1.   Analysis of the lithium resource market (Continued)

     (1)   鋰輝石精礦市場                             (1)   Market of spodumene concentrate

           澳大利亞作為全球最大的鋰礦石                            As one of the world’s largest producers of lithium ore,
           生產國之一，憑藉其成熟的礦業                            Australia continues to consolidate its core position in the
           基礎、完善的法律法規及良好的                            global spodumene supply chain by virtue of its mature mining
           配套設施，持續鞏固其在全球鋰                            foundation, comprehensive laws and regulations, and sound
           輝石供應鏈中的核心地位。根據                            supporting facilities. According to the data from Fastmarkets,
           Fastmarkets 的 數 據 ， 截 至 2026              as of June 2026, the domestic cost, insurance and freight
           年6月，5%–6%鋰輝石精礦的中                          (CIF) price of 5%-6% spodumene concentrate was around
           國 到 岸 價 格 約 合 2,150–2,270 美               USD2,150-2,270 per ton, representing an increase of 35%-
           元╱噸，較2026年初價格1,560–                       45% from the price of USD1,560-1,590 per ton at the beginning
           1,590美元╱噸上漲35%–45%，                       of 2026, reflecting an overall upward trend in lithium prices in
           反映出上半年鋰價整體走強的運                            the first half of the year. Australia, an important source of global
           行態勢。澳大利亞作為全球鋰礦                            lithium resources, has a relatively stable supply from major
           資源的重要產地，主要鋰礦生產                            lithium ore producers. In the first half of 2026, production at
           商的供應量相對穩定。2026年上                          major Australian mines saw a mix of increases and decreases,
           半年，澳大利亞主要礦山產量增                            with overall supply remaining largely stable. Some mines
           減並存，整體供應基本持穩。部                            lowered their production forecasts due to declining ore grades
           分礦山因礦石品位下降和回收率                            and fluctuations in recovery rates, while certain mines achieved
           波動而調低產量預期，一些礦山                            record-high quarterly output, and previously suspended
           則實現單季產量新高，另有停產                            projects resumed operations. Overall, following the downturn
           項目正重啟運營。整體來看，澳                            in lithium prices in the early stages, Australian mines have

           近年來，中國市場主要鋰化合物價格波                                  In recent years, prices of major lithium compounds in the Chinese
           動幅度較大。2026年上半年，中國主                                 market have experienced significant fluctuations. In the first half of
           要鋰化合物價格整體呈現先揚後抑的走                                  2026, prices for major lithium compounds in China generally followed
           勢。年初，受下游補庫需求拉動及供應                                  a pattern of rising first and then retreating. At the beginning of the
           端階段性偏緊影響，電池級碳酸鋰等產                                  year, driven by downstream restocking demand and periodic supply
           品價格穩步上行，市場情緒較為樂觀。                                  tightness, prices of products such as battery-grade lithium carbonate
           進入二季度，價格漲勢加速，5月份在                                  rose steadily, and market sentiment was relatively optimistic. Entering
           多重因素共振下觸及上半年高點。隨                                   the second quarter, the price uptrend accelerated, and in May, prices
           後，隨著部分新增產能釋放、進口礦供                                  reached their first-half highs driven by a confluence of multiple factors.
           應增加以及下游採購節奏放緩，市場供                                  Subsequently, as certain new production capacity came on stream,
           需關係逐步緩和，價格自高位快速回                                   imported ore supply increased and downstream procurement slowed,
           調，2026年上半年末較5月高點出現明                                market supply-demand conditions gradually eased, and prices
           顯回落。總體而言，2026年上半年鋰價                                retreated rapidly from elevated levels, with notable declines from highs
           經歷了從溫和上漲到快速拉升、繼而回                                  in May by the end of the first half of 2026. Overall, lithium prices in
           調修正的完整週期。市場核心矛盾依然                                  the first half of 2026 underwent a full cycle from moderate increases
           圍繞供需動態平衡展開，行業對中長期                                  to rapid surges, followed by corrections. The core market tension
           供需格局的判斷更趨理性，市場運行節                                  continues to revolve around the dynamic balance between supply
           奏較前期更加依賴於實際消費兌現和新                                  and demand. The industry’s assessment of the medium- to long-
           增產能投放進度。                                           term supply-demand landscape has become more rational, and the
                                                              pace of market movements has become more dependent on actual
                                                              consumption realisation and the pace of new capacity deployment
                                                              than in earlier periods.

           為最強勁的增量來源，需求佔比大幅躍                                  to a balanced push across both domestic and international markets;
           升，與海外市場共同構成行業增長的核                                  meanwhile, the energy storage sector has emerged as the strongest

           進以及新興增量需求的湧現，為產業長                                  core pillar of industry growth. Continued breakthroughs in new energy

           分受益於動力與儲能雙引擎驅動、需求                                  new incremental demand, provide a solid foundation for the industry’s

           政策端，以《新能源汽車廢舊動力電池                                  from capacity building to a new phase of institutionalised closed-

           施行為標誌，行業建立了全國溯源信息                                  implementation of the Interim Measures for the Recycling and
           平台與電池數字身份證制度，確立了車                                  Comprehensive Utilisation of Waste Power Batteries from New Energy
           電一體報廢規則及生產者延伸責任，實                                  Vehicles (《新能源汽車廢舊動力電池回收和綜合利用管理暫行辦法》),
           現了從宏觀規劃到強制性全鏈條管理的                                  the industry has established a national traceability information platform
           躍升；與此同時，主管部門啟動聯合執                                  and a digital battery identification system, instituted the vehicle-battery

           力。在行業端，回收利用骨幹企業體系                                  thereby achieving a significant leap from macro-level planning to

           下游協同效應進一步增強。總體來看，                                  capacity expansion and capital deployment, and the repeal of the

7.   低空經濟市場分析               7.   Analysis of the low-altitude economy market

     從2024年起，低空經濟已連續三年被寫         Since 2024, the low-altitude economy has been included in the
     入政府工作報告。2026年政府工作報告         government work report for three consecutive years. The 2026
     首次將其明確為國家「新興支柱產業」，          Government Work Report explicitly designated it as a national
     與集成電路、航空航天、生物醫藥並列           “emerging pillar industry” for the first time, placing it alongside
     納入戰略性新興產業核心佈局，標誌著           integrated circuits, aerospace, and biopharmaceuticals within the core
     產業正從概念探索加速邁入規模化發展           framework of strategic emerging industries. This marks the industry’s
     階段。                         accelerated transition from conceptual exploration to large-scale
                                 development.

     2026年上半年，低空經濟的政策與標          In the first half of 2026, the policy and standards framework for
     準體系加速完善。2026年2月，市場監         the low-altitude economy was rapidly strengthened. In February
     管總局會同中央空管辦、國家發展改革           2026, the State Administration for Market Regulation, together
     委等十部門聯合發佈《低空經濟標準體           with ten departments including the Central Air Traffic Management
     系建設指南（2025年版）》，重點圍繞低        Commission Office and the National Development and Reform
     空航空器、低空基礎設施、低空空中交           Commission, jointly issued the Guidelines for the Development of the
     通管理、安全監管和應用場景五大核心           Low-Altitude Economy Standards System (2025 Edition) (《低空經濟
     領域，構建「四維融合」標準供給體系，          標準體系建設指南(2025年版)》), focusing on five core areas – low-
     明確力爭到2027年基本建立低空經濟          altitude aircraft, low-altitude infrastructure, low-altitude air traffic
     標準體系，到2030年領域標準超過300        management, safety regulation and application scenarios – to establish
     項。將於2026年7月1日起施行的新修         a “four-dimensional integrated” standards supply system, specifying
     訂《中華人民共和國民用航空法》提出優          the goal of fundamentally establishing the low-altitude economy
     化低空空域資源配置，推動建設民用低           standards system by 2027 and exceeding 300 sector-specific
     空飛行和應用相關監管服務平台。與此           standards by 2030. The newly revised Civil Aviation Law of the
     同時，國家發展改革委低空經濟發展司           People’s Republic of China (《中華人民共和國民用航空法》), effective
     正式亮相，明確「十五五」期間推動低空          from 1 July 2026, calls for optimising the allocation of low-altitude
     經濟實現「管得好、飛得穩、用得活」；          airspace resources and promoting the development of regulatory and
     2026年5月，中國民航局「低空安全司」        service platforms related to civil low-altitude flight and applications.
     正式亮相，標誌著國家層面對低空經濟           Concurrently, the Department of Low-Altitude Economy Development
     的管理架構從「發展」與「安全」雙線並行         under the National Development and Reform Commission was
     走向制度化閉環。                    formally unveiled, setting out the objective of achieving “sound
                                 management, stable flight operations and flexible utilisation”
                                 for the low-altitude economy during the 15th Five-Year Plan period.
                                 In May 2026, the “Low-Altitude Safety Department” of the Civil
                                 Aviation Administration of China was formally established, marking the
                                 transition of the national-level management framework for the low-
                                 altitude economy from a dual-track approach of “development” and

     8.    固態電池市場分析                                      8.   Analysis of the solid-state battery market

           固態電池是一種新型儲能技術，其工作                                  Solid-state batteries represent a new type of energy storage
           原理與液態鋰離子電池相似。固態鋰離                                  technology, with operating principles similar to those of liquid lithium-
           子電池的主要材料包括正極材料、負極                                  ion batteries. The main materials of solid-state lithium-ion batteries
           材料和固態電解質等，其核心在於將液                                  include cathode materials, anode materials and solid electrolytes.
           態電池的電解液與隔膜替換成固態電解                                  The core of this technology lies in replacing the liquid electrolyte and
           質，實現不用或者少用隔膜及電解液，                                  separator of liquid batteries with solid-state electrolytes, thereby
           相較於傳統鋰離子電池具備更高能量密                                  reducing or eliminating the need for separators and liquid electrolytes.
           度（單位體積╱重量下存儲更多電能）、                                 Compared with traditional lithium-ion batteries, solid-state batteries
           更快的充電速度、更長的使用壽命以及                                  offer higher energy density (storing more electric energy per unit
           更高的安全性（避免液態電解液洩漏、                                  volume/weight), faster charging speeds, longer service life and
           燃燒風險），但目前這項技術仍面臨電                                  increased safety (mitigating risks of liquid electrolyte leakage and
           解質材料成本高、界面阻抗大、規模化                                  combustion). However, this technology currently faces challenges
           生產工藝不成熟等挑戰，被視為下一代                                  including high electrolyte material costs, significant interface
           電池技術的重要發展方向。                                       impedance and immature large-scale production processes. Solid-
                                                              state batteries are considered a key direction for the next generation
                                                              of battery technology development.

2.   鋰化工業務                                    2.    Lithium Chemical Business

     公司是全球最大的金屬鋰生產商、國內                              As the world’s largest metal lithium producer and the largest lithium
     最大的鋰化合物供應商，公司同時擁有                              compounds supplier in the PRC, the Company owns the industrialized
     「滷水提鋰」、「礦石提鋰」和「回收提鋰」                           technology of “lithium extracted from brine”, “lithium extracted from
     產業化技術。報告期內，新余贛鋒分別                              ore” and “lithium extracted from decommissioned batteries” at the
     將高純碳酸鋰產能升級至年產30,000                            same time. During the Reporting Period, Xinyu Ganfeng upgraded its
     噸、氟化鋰產能升級至年產25,000噸，                           high-purity lithium carbonate capacity to 30,000 tons per annum and
     均在試生產階段； 青海贛鋒一期年產                              its lithium fluoride capacity to 25,000 tons per annum, respectively,
     1,000噸金屬鋰項目處於試生產階段，                            both of which are at the trial production stage; Phase I of Qinghai
     預計2026年三季度正式投產。公司將                             Ganfeng’s 1,000 tons per annum lithium metal project was at the
     進一步做好精益生產、節能降耗工作，                              trial production stage and is expected to be officially commissioned
     主動向細處降成本，提升工廠數智化水                              in the third quarter of 2026. The Company will further implement
     平，加速培育新質生產力。                                   lean production, energy conservation and consumption reduction,
                                                    proactively reduce costs in details, improve the digital and intelligent
                                                    level of factories, and accelerate the cultivation of new quality
                                                    productive forces.

     3.    鋰資源                                           3.    Lithium Resources

           公司持續關注全球範圍內的優質資源及                                   The Company continuously monitored high-quality resources and
           配套項目，做好資源項目與生產項目的                                   supporting projects worldwide, ensuring the capacity matching
           產能配套，公司投產資源所在地已覆蓋                                   between resource projects and production projects. Its commissioned
           馬里、澳大利亞、阿根廷、中國，資源                                   resource locations covered Mali, Australia, Argentina, and China,
           種類包括鋰輝石、鹽湖及鋰雲母，通過                                   with resource types including spodumene, salars, and lepidolite. This
           構建多元化的資源供給體系，逐步加強                                   diversified resource supply system gradually enhanced the resilience
           公司的原材料供應鏈韌性。                                        of the Company’s raw material supply chain.

          統性技術突破。公司同步推進硅                          specific-energy, high-power batteries, achieving systematic
          碳與鋰金屬負極雙路線突破產業                          technological breakthroughs. The Company has simultaneously
          化門檻，以金屬鋰負極技術為                           advanced the industrialization threshold breakthrough of dual
          核心，推動高比能電池量產進                           routes: silicon-carbon and lithium metal anodes. With lithium
          程 。400Wh/kg 電 池 循 環 壽 命 已               metal anode technology as the core, it has promoted the mass
          突 破 1,100 次 並 完 成 工 程 驗 證 ，             production process of high-specific-energy batteries. The cycle
          具備規模化應用潛力；全球首款                          life of 400Wh/kg batteries has exceeded 1,100 cycles and
          500Wh/kg 級 10Ah 產 品 實 現 小 批             completed engineering verification, with large-scale application

                                                               corresponding hedging measures

           我們的業務位於中國內地，且所有交易                                   Our business is located in the Chinese mainland and all transactions
           以人民幣進行。除若干銀行結餘以美元                                   are denominated in RMB. Most of our assets and liabilities are
           及其他外幣計值外，大部分資產及負債                                   denominated in RMB, except for certain bank balances which were
           以人民幣計值。以美元計值的資產及負                                   denominated in U. S. dollars and other foreign currencies. Our assets
           債主要由若干於中國內地以外註冊成立                                   and liabilities denominated in U. S. dollars were mainly held by certain
           並以美元為其功能貨幣的附屬公司持                                    subsidiaries which were incorporated outside the Chinese mainland
           有，且我們於報告期內並無於中國內地                                   and adopted U. S. dollars as their functional currency, and we did not
           進行重大外匯交易。因此，我們於報告                                   conduct any material foreign exchange transactions in the Chinese
           期內並無重大外匯風險。                                         mainland during the Reporting Period. In view of the foregoing, we
                                                               had no material foreign exchange risks during the Reporting Period.

           為應對操作風險，本集團已經制訂了                                    To deal with the operation risks, the Group has prepared the Foreign
           《外匯套期保值管理制度》，規定金融衍                                  Exchange Hedging Management System (         《外匯套期保值管理制
           生品交易不得單純以盈利為目的，且只                                   度》), prescribing that transactions on financial derivatives shall not be
           能以集團自有資金開展。董事會已對外                                   conducted purely for profit and shall be carried out with the Group’s
           匯套期保值業務規模金額設定年度上                                    self-owned funds only. The Board has set an annual cap for the scale
           限，現階段已訂立部分具體交易內容涉                                   of such foreign exchange hedging business and some concrete
           及普通遠期業務。本集團會密切監察我                                   transactions are made for the moment involving ordinary forward

           風險。                                                 when necessary to help reduce foreign exchange risks.

     報告期內其他重大事項                                          Other Significant Events During the Reporting Period

     墨西哥Sonora項目的情況更新                                    Updates on Sonora Project in Mexico

     2024年5月，本公司旗下控股子公司贛鋒國                               In May 2024, Ganfeng International Trading (Shanghai) Co., Ltd., Bacanora
     際 貿 易（ 上 海 ）有 限 公 司 、Bacanora Lithium               Lithium Limited and Sonora Lithium Ltd. (“Claimants”), the controlled
     Limited、Sonora Lithium Ltd.（「申請人」）就墨                subsidiaries of the Company, initiated an arbitration proceeding against
     西哥違反投資條約損害彼等權利的若干舉措，                                Mexico before the International Centre for Settlement of Investment Disputes
     向國際投資爭端解決中心（「ICSID」）提請仲裁                            (“ICSID”) to challenge various measures that violated their rights under
     程序。爭議舉措包括墨西哥頒佈的一系列法律                                investment treaties. These measures included a series of laws, regulations,
     法規及相關舉措，這些法律法規及相關舉措實                                and related measures issued by Mexico, which effectively nationalized
     際上將鋰資源國有化，影響了Sonora Lithium                         lithium resources, impacted the operation of the Sonora Lithium Project
     項目（「項目」）的運營，並最終以虛假理由取消                              (“Project”) and culminated with the cancellation of the mineral concessions
     了本公司的墨西哥子公司持有的礦產特許權。                                held by the Company’s Mexican subsidiaries on pretextual grounds.

     報告期內，本次仲裁繼續按照國際投資爭端解                                During the Reporting Period, the arbitration continued to proceed in
     決中心(ICSID)仲裁程序推進。ICSID秘書長此                          accordance with the arbitration rules of the ICSID. The Secretary-General
     前已正式登記本次仲裁事項，仲裁庭於2025                               of ICSID had previously formally registered the arbitration, and the arbitral
     年1月正式成立；2025年4月，申請人向仲裁                              tribunal was formally constituted in January 2025. In April 2025, the
     庭提交仲裁陳述書、證人陳述及專家報告。                                 Claimants submitted the memorial, witness statements and expert reports
     申請人請求仲裁庭裁定墨西哥全面彌補其違                                 to the arbitral tribunal. The Claimants requested the arbitral tribunal to
     反相關條約義務所造成的損失，具體包括：(i)                              order Mexico to make full reparation for the losses caused by its breach
     恢復申請人在墨西哥開發項目的特許權及相                                 of relevant treaty obligations, which specifically includes: (i) restoring the
     關權利，並賠償項目延誤造成的損失；或(ii)按                             Claimants’ concessions and related rights to develop the Project in Mexico,
     照整個項目的公平市場價值向申請人支付賠                                 and compensating for the losses caused by the delay of the Project; or (ii)
     償。2025年7月，本公司的墨西哥子公司撤回                              paying compensation to the Claimants based on the fair market value of the
     了在墨西哥提起的憲法保護訴訟及行政訴訟。                                entire Project. In July 2025, the Company’s Mexican subsidiaries withdrew
     2026年5月，本案進入文件調取階段；截至                               the constitutional protection proceedings and administrative lawsuits filed
     2026年6月30日，雙方已分別提出文件調取請                             in Mexico. In May 2026, the case entered the document production phase;
     求，公司就對方提出的相關文件調取請求提交                                as of 30 June 2026, both parties had respectively submitted requests for
     反對意見，相關文件調取事項由仲裁庭進一步                                document production, and the Company had submitted objections to the
     審理決定。此次撤訴行為與申請人在ICSID仲                              relevant document production requests raised by the counterparty, with
     裁中尋求恢復性救濟的訴求相一致，不會對本                                the relevant document production matters to be further considered and
     公司及其子公司造成重大不利影響。目前，案                                determined by the arbitral tribunal. This withdrawal of lawsuits is consistent
     件尚未進入開庭審理階段，仲裁庭正在組織雙                                with the Claimants’ claims seeking restitutionary relief in the ICSID
     方完成證據收集和案件事實調查工作。                                   arbitration, and will not cause any material adverse impact on the Company
                                                         and its subsidiaries. Currently, the case has not yet entered the hearing
                                                         stage, and the arbitral tribunal is coordinating with both parties to complete
                                                         the evidence gathering and fact-finding procedures.

            (b)     鋰電池板塊： 生產及銷售鋰電                            (b)   Lithium battery segment: manufacture and sale of lithium
                    池，並提供工商業儲能服務；                                   batteries, and rendering of storage at users’ end services;

                    售。

            管理層對本集團各經營板塊的經營業績                                 Management monitors the results of the Group’s operating segments
            分開管理，以此決定資源分配及業績評                                 separately for the purpose of making decisions about resource
            估。板塊業績基於各項須予呈報的板塊                                 allocation and performance assessment. Segment performance
            溢利╱虧損進行評價，該板塊溢利╱虧                                 is evaluated based on reportable segment profit/loss, which is a
            損以經調整的除稅前溢利╱虧損進行計                                 measure of adjusted profit/loss before tax. The adjusted profit/loss
            量。該經調整的除稅前溢利╱虧損將利                                 before tax is measured consistently with the Group’s profit before tax
            息收入及與租賃無關的融資成本排除在                                 except that interest income and non-lease related finance costs are
            外。除此以外，其計量與本集團之除稅                                 excluded from such measurement.

  考慮股票期權計劃時，每股攤薄盈利金                          per share amount is increased when taking the share option scheme

  年6月30日止六個月的每股基本盈利具                         the basic earnings per share during the six months ended 30 June
  有反稀釋作用，並在計算每股攤薄盈利                          2025, and were ignored in the calculation of diluted earnings per
  時被忽略。因此，每股攤薄盈利金額乃                          share. Therefore, the diluted earnings per share amounts are based
  基於期內虧損及歸屬於持續經營的虧損                          on the loss for the period and the loss attributable to continuing
  人民幣536,213,000元以及截至2025年6                  operations of RMB536,213,000 and the weighted average number of
  月30日止六個月已發行普通股加權平均                         ordinary shares of 2,004,568,550 outstanding during the six months
  數2,004,568,550股。                           ended 30 June 2025.

  於自願交易雙方（而非強迫或清算出售）                     at the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a

 上市股權投資的公允價值以市場報價為                       The fair values of listed equity investments are based on quoted
 基礎。指定為按公允價值計入其他全面                       market prices. The fair values of unlisted equity investments
 收益的非上市股權投資的公允價值乃根                       designated at fair value through other comprehensive income have
 據市場估值技術估計，該估值方法乃基                       been estimated using a market-based valuation technique based on
 於不受可觀察市場價格或利率所支持的                       assumptions that are not supported by observable market prices or
 假設。估值要求董事根據行業、規模、                       rates. The valuation requires the directors to determine comparable
 槓桿作用及策略釐定可資比較公眾公司                       public companies (peers) based on industry, size, leverage and
 （同業），並就每一識別的可資比較公司                      strategy, and to calculate an appropriate price multiple, such as
 計算適當的價格倍數，如企業價值╱息                       enterprise value to earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and
 稅折舊攤銷前利潤（「EV/EBITDA」）倍數                 amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”) multiple and price to earnings (“P/E”)
 及市盈率（「市盈率」）倍數。該等倍數乃                     multiple, for each comparable company identified. The multiple
 按可資比較公司的企業價值除以盈利計                       is calculated by dividing the enterprise value of the comparable
 量計算。交易倍數其後根據公司特定事                       company by an earnings measure. The trading multiple is then
 實及情況就可比較公司之間流動性不足                       discounted for considerations such as illiquidity and size differences
 及規模差異等考慮因素予以折現。貼現                       between the comparable companies based on company-specific
 倍數適用於非上市股權投資的相應盈利                       facts and circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the
 計量，以計量公允價值。董事認為，計                       corresponding earnings measure of the unlisted equity investments
 入綜合財務狀況表的由估值技術產生的                       to measure the fair value. The directors believe that the estimated
 估算公允價值及計入其他全面收益的公                       fair values resulting from the valuation technique, which are recorded
 允價值相關變動屬合理，並認為該等項                       in the consolidated statement of financial position, and the related

                                         income, are reasonable, and that they were the most appropriate
                                         values at the end of the reporting period.

             於 2026 年 8 月 24 日 ，GFL International                 On 24 August 2026, GFL International Co., Limited (“GFL
             Co., Limited（「 GFL International 」）與                 International”) and LAR entered into the Convertible Debenture
             LAR訂立可轉債認購協議，據此，GFL                                  Subscription Agreement, pursuant to which GFL International agreed
             International同意以自有資金1.8億美元                           to subscribe a 6-year term unsecured convertible debenture issued
             認購LAR發行的無擔保6年期可轉換債                                   by LAR with its self-owned funds of USD180 million to support
             券，以支持LAR的資金需求，包括償還                                   LAR’s funding needs, including the repayment of its prior debts.
             其前期債務。GFL International持有LAR                         GFL International holds 9.7% of equity interest in LAR. Assuming the
             9.7%的股權。假設可轉債獲全數轉換，                                  Convertible Debenture is fully converted, GFL International will hold
             按完全攤薄基準計算，GFL International                          approximately 16.1% of the equity interests in LAR on a fully diluted
             將持有LAR約16.1%的股權。                                     basis.
